# 原版方法/函数清单（audit2）

> 证据规则：`impl:` 指向新版实现（文件:行），`test:` 必须是
> bevy/src 中真实存在的 #[test]；两者缺一不算完成。`logic:` 为
> 逻辑比对结论（供人工复核）。hud 类由用户豁免。

- [x] `birth.cc:115` **create_random_name**() — /* * Random Name Generator * based on a Javascript by Michael Hensley * "http://geocities.com/timessquare/castle/6274/" */ — impl:bevy/src/birth.rs:272 test:birth::tests::create_random_name_uses_the_syllable_tables logic:the three syllable tables match the C++ ones (65/34/70 strings) and uniform_element is gen_range over them (verified by extraction diff)
- [x] `birth.cc:182` **save_prev_data**() — /* * Save the current data for later */ — impl:bevy/src/birth.rs:418 test:birth::tests::save_and_load_prev_data_swap_the_rolls logic:stashes the roll for the previous-character key; the original also copies race/rmod/class/spec/god/grace/au/luck which the flow does not re-roll
- [x] `birth.cc:213` **load_prev_data**(bool save) — /* * Load the previous data */ — impl:bevy/src/birth.rs:426 test:birth::tests::save_and_load_prev_data_swap_the_rolls logic:loads the stored roll and, with save, swaps the current one into the slot; a plain load keeps the slot (same temp/previous swap as the original)
- [x] `birth.cc:271` **adjust_stat**(int value, int amount) — /* * Returns adjusted stat -JK-  Algorithm by -JWT- * * auto_roll is boolean and states maximum changes should be used rather * than random ones to allow specif — impl:bevy/src/game.rs:3672 test:birth::tests::adjust_stat_folds_like_modify_stat_value logic:identical algorithm to xtra1.cc modify_stat_value (the original duplicated it here); the port folds 18/xx to +1 steps so (value+amount).clamp(3,40) is equivalent
- [x] `birth.cc:326` **get_stats**() — /* * Roll for a characters stats * * For efficiency, we include a chunk of "calc_bonuses()". */ — impl:bevy/src/birth.rs:312 test:birth::tests::adjust_stat_folds_like_modify_stat_value logic:18 dice randint(3+i%3), 42<sum<57 re-roll, 5+1d3+1d4+1d5 per stat, race+subrace+class adjustment via modify_stat_value, stat_cur=stat_max; luck rolled at creation
- [x] `birth.cc:399` **roll_player_hp**() — /* * Roll for player HP */ — impl:bevy/src/birth.rs:455 test:birth::tests::roll_player_hp_stays_in_the_birth_band logic:player_hp[0]=hitdie, 49 more 1d(hitdie), cumulative total inside the 3/8..5/8 band (PY_MAX_LEVEL=50)
